Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in wind theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 609 and the invariant I(A,B) = 219 mod 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 609 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the wind structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 219. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
